## Appendix A — per-group audit notes (coverage evidence)

Each group returned a per-file verdict. Files listed here were read in full; "no findings" means no defect survived that group's own re-check.

### A1-core-tick

Confirmed findings from this group: 5

Files read: `src/game/gameTick.ts`, `src/game/tickLayerRealtime.ts`, `src/game/tickLayerSystems.ts`, `src/game/tickLayerAssign.ts`, `src/game/tickLayerDaily.ts`, `src/game/simFocus.ts`, `src/game/simHelpers.ts`, `src/game/gameLoop.ts`, `src/game/gameEngine.ts`, `src/game/dayCycle.ts`, `src/game/dayCycleClock.ts`, `src/game/entityTypeCache.ts`, `src/game/simulation/simulationInvariants.ts`, `src/game/simulation/simInvariants.ts`, `src/game/simulation/simulationTypes.ts`, `src/game/simulation/decisionRegistry.ts`, `src/game/residencyReconciliation.ts`, `src/game/residencyOccupancy.ts`, `src/game/humanLifecycleCleanup.ts`, `src/game/dailyBuildingEconomy.ts`, `src/game/dailyPopulation.ts`, `src/game/entityFactory.ts`, `src/game/simRng.ts`, `src/game/entityCounts.ts`, `src/game/saveSchema.ts`, `src/game/simWorker/simPrep.ts`, `src/game/simWorker/GameWorkerHost.ts`, `src/game/simWorker/gameWorker.ts`, `src/game/simWorker/commands.ts`, `docs/archive/SIMULATION_AUTHORITY.md`, `tests/gameTick.layerOrder.test.ts`, `tests/dayCycle.cadence.test.ts`, `tests/deterministicSeed.test.ts`, `tests/moonHowler.byTypeReuse.test.ts`, `tests/fullYear.integration.test.ts`, `tests/simulation.invariants.test.ts`, `tests/gameLoop.commandDispatch.test.ts`, `tests/gameLoop.diagnostics.test.ts`

VERDICT PER FILE
- src/game/gameTick.ts — layer order/cadence matches SIMULATION_AUTHORITY §4 and tests/gameTick.layerOrder.test.ts (72/4/18/72, realtime first); calendar rollover, deathsThisTick/untracked-spawn cache invalidation and the entityByType rebuild conditions verified correct. Only the winter-heating persistence gap (F1) and a no-op scan (notes) found.
- src/game/tickLayerRealtime.ts — grid sync order, moon-howler byType/playerHumans/predator refresh, bounded populationHistory/particle/text buffers all consistent; no finding (the Moon-Howler gated residence re-sync is discussed below).
- src/game/tickLayerSystems.ts — multi-tick step scaling is correct for energy, flash, combatTicks, reproduction cooldown, breeding probability and wander probability; predation/counter-attack/forgiveness paths unindex and grid-sync their casualties. Findings F2 (off-screen throttle) and F5 (unreachable growth branch).
- src/game/tickLayerAssign.ts — 18-tick = 4x/day cadence correct; filters dead entities via prepareWorkforce/assignMissingResidences internals; passes playerHumans as assignees and all Humans as genealogy; no finding.
- src/game/tickLayerDaily.ts — composition is once-per-day and does not duplicate another owner (friendships/feuds in relationships.ts, youth love and conception in humanRelationships.ts, leadership in leaderHouse/villageLeadership, requests in groupEvents via dailyWorldEvents). Finding F6 (dead guards only).
- src/game/simFocus.ts — focus math, EntityType bucketing and draw-bucket construction are correct and total over EntityType; only the OFFSCREEN_WILDLIFE_THROTTLE unit mismatch (half of F2).
- src/game/simHelpers.ts — getSeason/seasonBlendForDay/getMultiplier/addReputation/getPollutionProductionMultiplier match their documented formulas and clamps; no finding.
- src/game/gameLoop.ts — worker protocol, optimistic-command replacement/revert and worker-authoritative delta handling match the §2 amendment; findings F3, F4, F7.
- src/game/gameEngine.ts — pure compatibility barrel; verified the re-exported modules/symbols exist and the docstring matches intent. No findings (it does publish tickWildlife/tickGrassDaily as public entry points — see below).

DID NOT RUN VERIFICATION: this was a read-only audit; I did not execute the test suite, typecheck or a build, so no pass/fail claim is made. Every claim above is from reading the code and its call sites; the tests listed in filesReviewed were read only to identify intentional behavior (notably tests/gameTick.layerOrder.test.ts, which locks 72/4/18 and the four-layer order, and tests/dayCycle.cadence.test.ts, which locks OFFSCREEN_HUMAN_THROTTLE = 8*TICKS_PER_HOUR).

UNRESOLVED / DESIGN QUESTIONS
1. Worker mode loses seeded determinism. Only worldGen.initGame calls setSimSeed + enableSeededGlobalRandom (worldGen.ts:568-570) and src/game/simWorker/** contains no simRng reference. Because the worker only ever receives a world via importSave/syncWorld, every Math.random() in tickLayerSystems (wolf recruitment line 104, breeding 706/720, wander 575-576, howl lines 474/521) and tickMoonHowlerCycle is the unseeded native generator in production worker mode, while the same code is seeded in main-thread mode and in tests/deterministicSeed.test.ts. Needs an owner decision (seed the worker on 'init' with the world seed) — reported as a note because the fix belongs to the worker entry point, not to this group's files.
2. tickLayerRealtime.ts:117-123 re-derives every residence's occupants from residenceBuildingId on every tick while a Moon Howler is in werewolf form (an O(population) scan in the realtime path). I could not find a case where the assign layer actually leaves it stale for a transformed howler (transformToWerewolfForm clears residenceBuildingId, and moonHowler itself re-syncs on transform/revert/cure), except a legacy save loaded mid-full-moon-night where migrateLegacyMoonHowler leaves a cursed Werewolf holding a residenceBuildingId; in that case this block is the only repair because tickLayerAssign's bucket is Humans-only. Confirm whether that legacy repair is the intent (if not, the block is redundant per-tick assignment work).
3. gameTick.ts:107-111 re-derives entity.age for cursed humans on every tick (full alive scan) although humanTick.ts:292-303 is the daily age owner and, for the adults that can be cursed (canMoonHowlerCurse requires age >= 18), the school-age multiplier it drops cannot apply, so the write is numerically a no-op. Not reported as a finding (no behavioural difference found), but it looks like leftover code from an older form-swap bug and could be deleted or scoped to the transformed entity.
4. gameLoop.applyAction's closure branch (gameLoop.ts:601-626) is unreachable from shipped UI: every applyGameAction call site passes a WorkerCommand (all 40+ in App.tsx, plus useVirtualPlayer). With a worker active it is rejected with a console error, so the legacy path is effectively dead; and isSimWorkerBooting() (gameLoop.ts:410) has no consumer, which is what makes finding F4 reachable.
5. simWorker/simPrep.ts does not back up populationHistory or yearlyStats/lifetimeStats, so a rolled-back worker tick keeps a spurious population sample and (at a year boundary) a duplicated yearly stat. Not reported because a tick fault leads GameLoop to dispose the worker and re-authoritative from the main-thread world, so the half-rolled-back worker state is discarded; worth a defensive addition if rollback is ever used to keep the worker alive.
6. Question of intent for simFocus.seasonBlendForDay: the `until <= 0` skip means the exact season-boundary day returns null (no blend) even though it is 'within blendDays' of the boundary; the renderer (renderer/terrain.ts:130-132) then bakes the pure new-season palette, which is visually continuous, so I treated it as intentional.

NO ISSUES FOUND (verified, worth recording): the byType WeakMap cache cannot go stale across command/delta boundaries (all command implementations structuredClone the world, and gameTick rebuilds on death/spawn/type-change/day); killHuman/death cleanup keeps occupants, partnerId and childrenIds consistent with the fact that gameTick drops dead entities from state.entities each tick; workforce/residence helpers all re-filter `alive` so the tick-start playerHumans snapshot used by the assign layer cannot assign a corpse; road/bridge/well/hospital flags only count completed, non-rival buildings; markWildlifeDead splices byType and grid entries so the hunt/mate/graze queries cannot pick a corpse.

### A10-human-subsystems-care

Confirmed findings from this group: 7

Files read: `src/game/simulation/humanLifecycle.ts`, `src/game/simulation/humanMovement.ts`, `src/game/simulation/humanNeeds.ts`, `src/game/simulation/humanSocial.ts`, `src/game/humanLifecycleCleanup.ts`, `src/game/relationships.ts`, `src/game/socialLife.ts`, `src/game/relationshipDiagnostics.ts`, `src/game/humanStatus.ts`, `src/game/citizenId.ts`, `src/game/citizenOverview.ts`, `src/game/familyTree.ts`, `src/game/familyLegacy.ts`, `src/game/hospitalCare.ts`, `src/game/hotelStay.ts`, `src/game/education.ts`, `src/game/apprenticeships.ts`, `src/game/skills.ts`, `src/game/settlerTraits.ts`

UNRESOLVED / NEEDS A DESIGN DECISION
1. Who owns the per-tick position integration for pathing entities? `steerWithPath` both moves the entity and leaves vx/vy set (pathfinding.ts:341-346) while its own docstring (line 298) tells callers not to move it again. humanMovement.ts:150 returns false on 'path' and humanTick.ts:1467 then integrates vx/vy again (road multiplier included), so commuting settlers also advance ~2x on blocked routes; hotelStay adds an explicit third move. I reported only the hotelStay instance (unambiguous inside one function); the humanMovement/humanTick half needs one decision: make steerWithPath set velocity only, or zero vx/vy after a 'path' result.
2. moonHowlerSaved omits pregnancyDueProgress (moonHowler.ts:336-361, restored at 422-424), so a cursed pregnant settler returns pregnant with no due date. Which owner repairs it (howler snapshot vs pregnancy owner) is a design call; I reported the humanLifecycle fallback because that line is in this group, but the root cause is outside it.
3. hospitalCare.findStaffedHospital (hospitalCare.ts:13-21) has no importer anywhere in src/ or tests/; humanTick builds its staffedHospitals list inline (humanTick.ts:248-252). Dead export — delete it or route humanTick through it.
4. Whether a stillbirth or a Wildkin birth should count in birthsCompletedThisInterval is a diagnostics/product decision (my finding 7 assumes the documented "only at a completed birth" reading).
5. Doctor treatment cadence needs a designer decision: is treatment a continuous per-tick effect (then scale it by PER_TICK_RATE_SCALE) or a per-hour action (then gate it on isStartOfClockHour)?
6. Not reported: `apprenticeships` clears master.apprenticeId when the apprentice is missing, but the existing-apprentice path (lines 62-66) never re-checks ownership, so a pair whose owner-side link was cleared can be taught by two masters; and an apprentice who turns adult is still taught forever. Not reachable from current writers (apprenticeId/apprenticeOfId are only set together), so I left it out. Likewise skills.getWorkerSkillMultiplier divides by the raw occupants length, so stale ids dilute the average, and settlerTraits.pickTrait falls back to the male-leaning weight set when gender is undefined (never called that way today).

SAVE PERSISTENCE: no gap found for this group. Entities and buildings are persisted whole (WORLD_STATE_SAVE_KEYS 'entities'/'buildings'), so friendships/feuds/traits/schoolDays/schoolTicksToday/educated/apprenticeId(+OfId)/hotelGuestIds/hotelStayBuildingId survive; the tick-valued fields these files write (griefUntilTick, hotelStayUntilTick, pregnancyProgress inside moonHowlerSaved) are scaled by migrateTickTimeline (saveLoad.ts:236-252); simDelta sends full aliveEntities (simDelta.ts:286), so those entity fields reach the main thread.

PER-FILE VERDICT
- simulation/humanLifecycle.ts — the birth owner is well structured and clears pregnancy state correctly, but I found the posthumous-bastard mislabel, the unsynced newborn residence, the misplaced births diagnostic, the due-progress fallback and unseeded birth rolls.
- simulation/humanMovement.ts — deterministic helpers; movement math correct; only the cross-file 'path' integration question (notes 1) remains.
- simulation/humanNeeds.ts — meal rule, exhaustion death, hunt yield and energy-loss modifiers all behave as their comments promise; no findings.
- simulation/humanSocial.ts — correct; lower-id pair roll prevents same-tick double fire; no findings.
- humanLifecycleCleanup.ts — death cleanup is otherwise sound (occupants, widowing, grief, orphan adoption); the reconcileOrphanedMarriages guard cannot repair the 'married' + no-partner state, and reconcileFamilyReferencesAfterRemoval erases an unborn child's father id.
- relationships.ts — daily pulse respects the documented pair budget and is symmetric; friendships are never pruned, so dead friends keep buffing energy.
- socialLife.ts — opinionated but internally consistent per-person day rolls; no findings.
- relationshipDiagnostics.ts — counters/snapshot semantics match the documented truth rule; module-global state is worker-local and unused by src consumers; no findings.
- humanStatus.ts — read-only projection, no sim writes, no findings.
- citizenId.ts — no findings.
- citizenOverview.ts — presentation-only aggregation; no findings.
- familyTree.ts — pure reads; no findings.
- familyLegacy.ts — pure reads; no findings.
- hospitalCare.ts — three findings (grief tick comparison, per-tick treatment magnitudes, doctorPresent conflation).
- hotelStay.ts — the path-branch double move; also its checkInVisitor JSDoc still claims "Charges gold once" while the body states "Free lodging for now" (stale contract text only, no gold system involved).
- education.ts — school-day crediting, tiers and graduation gates behave as documented; no findings (tier-1 boundary uses a literal 30 where other thresholds are named constants).
- apprenticeships.ts — claimed-set and graduation handling match the docstring; no findings (see notes 6).
- skills.ts — gainSkill/decay/reward behave as documented; no findings (see notes 6).
- settlerTraits.ts — trait rolling, opposites and inheritance caps match the tests; no findings (see notes 6).

### A11-human-behaviors-schedule

Confirmed findings from this group: 7

Files read: `src/game/humanChat.ts`, `src/game/humanHuntingBehavior.ts`, `src/game/humanLeisureBehavior.ts`, `src/game/humanVenueBehavior.ts`, `src/game/humanHospitalBehavior.ts`, `src/game/humanPatrolBehavior.ts`, `src/game/huntingSpots.ts`, `src/game/huntvisuals.ts`, `src/game/humanSchedule.ts`, `src/game/workSchedule.ts`, `src/game/venueSchedule.ts`, `src/game/scheduleFatigue.ts`, `src/game/scheduleFeedback.ts`, `src/game/dailyScheduleFatigue.ts`

VERDICT PER FILE (all 14 assigned files read in full; supporting files read for cross-reference: gameTick.ts, humanTick.ts, townHall.ts, hospitalCare.ts, simulationEntities.ts, dayCycle.ts/dayCycleClock.ts, spatialGrid.ts, saveSchema.ts/saveLoad.ts, speciesConfig.ts, ecologyStage.ts, dialogueTrees.ts, simQueries.ts, tests/*).

- humanChat.ts — session/tree bookkeeping is otherwise sound (advanceDialogue, role handoff, per-line tick conversion all match humanChat.duration + ambientPairing + dialogueBusy tests), but sayHumanChatPhrase leaks/orphans session entries (finding 3). Minor: ticksForDialogueLine is unclamped, so a long tree line exceeds the '2.5-5 game hours' comment at lines 66-68 (up to ~12h at the 38-char bubble cap); not reported because no caller reaches the documented bound in the dialogue bank.
- humanHuntingBehavior.ts — kill/resource/cleanup path is coherent (markWildlifeDead + clearHuntersTargetingPrey + addResource, consistent with tests/huntingSpot.cleanup and tests/huntLogic). Both valley-stage chatter gates (lines 184-190, 193-199) read raw `state.valleyStage` while ValleyEcology.ENABLED is false (gameConstants.ts:148) and ecologyStage.parkValleyStageState pins that field to 'stable', so they can never fire today; if the switch is flipped they become a per-tick retrigger of sayHumanChatPhrase (same class as findings 1/5) and would orphan dialogue sessions. Not reported as a finding because the branch is intentionally dormant.
- humanLeisureBehavior.ts — motive/steering logic is coherent (staggered social query, spouse injection, suppressIdle propagation). One unseeded roll (finding 6).
- humanVenueBehavior.ts — tavern service wrapper is correct (`workplace` is already narrowed to the tavern by isInnkeeper/onTavernShift before line 50); the free-time civic petition call is the only defect (finding 1). Its pre-existing daily twin is townHall.ts:345.
- humanHospitalBehavior.ts — PREGNANCY_TICKS * 0.85 threshold is correct (pregnancyProgress is an absolute 0..PREGNANCY_TICKS counter, humanLifecycle.ts:63); the redundant `|| entity.pregnant` in shouldAttemptHospitalTreatment is harmless (needsMedicalCare already returns true for pregnancy). Two defects: double movement (finding 2) and per-tick treatment (finding 5).
- humanPatrolBehavior.ts — logically correct and deterministic (Set iteration in insertion order, one log per newly-revealed group). It does perform an O(all humans) scan per barracks guard per tick with a second full scan per detected group, with no spatial-grid query, which conflicts with SIMULATION_AUTHORITY.md §9's 'New spatial queries use the spatial grid' checklist item; not reported as a finding because I have no correctness claim.
- huntingSpots.ts — prototype-chain validation bug (finding 7). Otherwise data-only.
- huntvisuals.ts — clean. Wall-clock retention is deliberate and locked by tests/huntVisuals.lifecycle; in-place compaction and the 8-entry cap are correct; pruning is called from tickLayerRealtime (sim side), not the renderer.
- humanSchedule.ts — no logic error found; every band of prefersHomeTonightFor is reachable and the weekend band is the only fall-through for the work window. buildWorkHours/isActiveFreeDay/formatHour/isOnWorkShiftFor/isTavernOpen/isOnInnkeeperShift have no callers anywhere in the repo (only re-exported by dayCycle.ts), i.e. dead exports — noted, not reported.
- workSchedule.ts — validation, normalization, immutability and start-tick math are correct (startHour 0-23, endHour<=startHour rejected, 2-16h bounds, TICKS_PER_DAY modulus exact).
- venueSchedule.ts — correct; the start-tick test on `tick % (24*TICKS_PER_HOUR)` matches TICKS_PER_DAY. I checked the `structuredClone(state)` in setVenueSchedule (used on the authoritative world from simWorker/commands.ts:421): it is safe because EntitySpatialGrid/RoadAvoidanceIndex are classes whose methods live on the prototype, and the next tick repairs/re-instantiates them (resolveSpatialGrid's `instanceof` check at spatialGrid.ts:447, gameTick's `typeof state.roadAvoidance.isNearRoad !== 'function'` rebuild at gameTick.ts:154, ScentGrid.adoptRuntime at scentGrid.ts:327).
- scheduleFatigue.ts — matches scheduleFatigue.test.ts; the `entity.faction` guard in recordScheduleWorkTick correctly excludes only visitor/rival/trade_caravan (Entity.faction has no 'player' value), and every settler that accrues ticks is covered by the daily reset for living non-juvenile player humans.
- scheduleFeedback.ts — exclusion-based type set (finding 4). Warning strings and durationDelta are otherwise correct.
- dailyScheduleFatigue.ts — correct: the two event filters (fatigueAfter > before with workedHours > 0, and the reverse) are mutually exclusive, averages guard the empty list, and the MEANINGFUL_FATIGUE_CHANGE pre-filter cannot drop a settler from both lists.

UNRESOLVED QUESTIONS / DESIGN DECISIONS:
1. Two 'default work window' sources coexist in this group: workSchedule.DEFAULT_WORK_SCHEDULE is 07-16 (9h, matched to STANDARD_PRODUCTION_WORK_HOURS) while humanSchedule.LEGACY_WINDOW/WORK_START/WORK_END are 07-18 (11h) and CHANGELOG.md:126 states the ordinary-work default is 07:00-18:00. The legacy window still drives school hours (humanTick.ts:542 isOnWorkShift), town-hall open hours (townHall.ts:218 isWorkHour), the marriage gate (humanRelationships.ts:1325 allowSocialLife) and the home icon/affair gate (humanRelationships.ts:205/252 and renderer/humans.ts:197-198 shouldBeAtHome). With the shipped 07-16 default a settler free-roaming at 17:00 is drawn with the home icon and is treated as 'at home' for affairs. Which window is authoritative after the ordinary-work feature, and should those call sites pass the configured schedule?
2. humanPatrolBehavior's unindexed per-tick population scan: acceptable, or should it move behind the existing staggered/spatial-query pattern?
3. If ValleyEcology.ENABLED is ever flipped true, humanHuntingBehavior.ts:184-199 need the same per-day gating fix as findings 1/5 before the chatter goes live.
4. No existing test covers the petition, hospital-treatment, sayHumanChatPhrase-session or scheduleFeedback-preview paths, so none of the reported findings are locked in by a passing test.

### A12-economy-production

Confirmed findings from this group: 5

Files read: `src/game/dailyBuildingEconomy.ts`, `src/game/economy.ts`, `src/game/economyLedger.ts`, `src/game/workshopEconomy.ts`, `src/game/workshops.ts`, `src/game/forge.ts`, `src/game/resourceCost.ts`, `src/game/resourceTypes.ts`, `src/game/resourceUtils.ts`, `src/game/research.ts`, `src/game/dayCycle.ts`, `src/game/dayCycleClock.ts`, `src/game/gameTick.ts`, `src/game/tickLayerDaily.ts`, `src/game/tickLayerSystems.ts`, `src/game/tickLayerAssign.ts`, `src/game/workforce.ts`, `src/game/skills.ts`, `src/game/workSchedule.ts`, `src/game/scheduleFatigue.ts`, `src/game/adjacencyIndex.ts`, `src/game/townHall.ts`, `src/game/education.ts`, `src/game/buildingPlacementActions.ts`, `src/game/buildings.ts`, `src/game/gameTypes.ts`, `src/game/worldGen.ts`, `src/game/saveLoad.ts`, `src/game/dashboardData.ts`, `src/game/simWorker/simPrep.ts`, `src/game/simWorker/gameWorker.ts`, `src/game/simBuffers/simDelta.ts`, `src/game/simulation/decisionRegistry.ts`, `src/game/playerHuman.ts`, `src/game/simRng.ts`, `src/components/tabPanels/VillageTabPanel.tsx`, `src/components/SelectedBuildingPanel.tsx`, `tests/economyAudit.storageCaps.test.ts`, `tests/dayCycle.cadence.test.ts`, `tests/weatherConsequences.test.ts`, `tests/electionGossip.dedup.test.ts`, `tests/huntingSpot.cleanup.test.ts`, `tests/mineOreMode.test.ts`, `tests/deterministicSeed.test.ts`, `tests/virtualPlayer.test.ts`

VERDICT PER FILE
- src/game/dailyBuildingEconomy.ts — owners are in the right place and cadence (daily layer, isProductionTick gates match PRODUCTION_INTERVAL, forge/blueberry/spoilage delegated); defects are the ledger amounts for hunting/fishing, the unrecorded greenhouse, the unfiltered Town Hall villager list, and the never-called cap owner it should invoke.
- src/game/economy.ts — updateStorageCaps is correct in isolation but has no production caller (highest-impact finding of the group); trade-route merge and spoilage/consume helpers are sound.
- src/game/economyLedger.ts — sound; day-keyed lazy rollover, 30-day prune, amount<=0 guard all correct; only defect is upstream callers passing uncapped amounts and skipping the greenhouse.
- src/game/workshopEconomy.ts — read-only estimate, no mutation; formula diverges from the production owner (work-hour/fatigue), docstring says 'daily' while it actually estimates one 2-day pulse (the UI label is right, so that part is only doc drift).
- src/game/workshops.ts — no findings; recipe index, fallback, formatRecipeInputs and canAffordWorkshopRecipe behave as named (formatRecipeInputs covers all five keys).
- src/game/forge.ts — no findings. Queue-time consumption is protected against double-charge (L267 returns originalState when the same order is already active, and getForgeBlockReason returns null for it only after payment); normalize/migrate/tick cadence (workshop interval = 2 days, ~6 days per order) matches the research notification text; progress clamped.
- src/game/resourceCost.ts — no findings; empty-cost formatting is explicit ('Free') and canAffordResourceCost filters to positive amounts.
- src/game/resourceTypes.ts — no findings for current callers; `hasEnoughResources` uses truthiness (`if (cost.wood && ...)`) so a negative cost entry would read as affordable, but the function has no callers anywhere (verified repo-wide) so there is no reachable failure to report.
- src/game/resourceUtils.ts — no findings. `addCappedResource` returns the true added amount (the property the workshop/ledger paths depend on); deduct/consume clamp at 0; getStorageCap falls back to Infinity only if storageMax is absent/NaN (saveLoad always supplies one).
- src/game/research.ts — start/complete/unlock flow is single-owner, deduped and read by getMultiplier via `researched`; only the wood/stone/gold-only cost gate is a (currently latent) contract gap.

DROPPED CANDIDATES (verified, judged below the reporting bar — not silent findings)
1. dailyBuildingEconomy.ts:167 `const wasCompleted = building.completed;` is always false by the enclosing guard (`!building.completed && progress < 100`) — dead local, but the value passed to syncAdjacency is the correct one (building was not completed before), so there is no behavioural impact.
2. dailyBuildingEconomy.ts:793-796 `updatedBuildings.find((b) => b.completed && (b.type === TownHall || b.type === House))` may anchor the '+rep (roads)' floating text on a rival camp's House (rival Houses exist, groupEvents.ts:448, faction 'rival'). Reachable only for a player with roads + Urban Planning but no completed House/Town Hall, and the impact is a misplaced label, so it is cosmetic.
3. Store production (L540) is the only production block whose yield does not scale with `workers` while still requiring `workers > 0`; no comment or caller declares a flat Store yield, so this may be intended tuning rather than an omission — needs a design answer, do not 'fix' blind.
4. Passive buildings that are not in BUILDING_JOB_TYPES (Silo) bypass the `staffed` requirement, so a *rival* Silo would feed the player's stores; verified unreachable today because pickRivalExpansionType (rivalEvents.ts:82-119) and the founding offsets never create Barn/Silo/WoodStorehouse. Worth a cheap `building.faction !== 'rival'` guard if rival building types ever expand.
5. tickBuildingProgress calls assignMissingWorkers (workforce/assignment owner) twice per day in addition to the assign layer's 4x/day pulse (tickLayerAssign.ts:37); this is deliberate and commented ('Fill crews before progress so unfinished sites always get hands on site this day') and is needed so a building that completes inside the daily layer is staffed the same day, so it is not reported as a cadence violation.
6. Dead export surface in resourceUtils.ts (hasEnoughResources, consumeResources, deductResource, isResourceCapped) and forge.isBlacksmithStaffed have no callers; harmless API surface (unlike updateStorageCaps, which hides a missing gameplay effect), so only noted.
7. tickElectionGossip being driven from tickStaticDaily (dailyBuildingEconomy.ts:241) was checked against tests/electionGossip.dedup.test.ts: the daily call is the intended single roll and the ceremony skip is pinned by that test — not a duplicate-cadence bug.

UNRESOLVED / DESIGN QUESTIONS
- Finding 1 hinges on whether per-building storage caps are still intended. Evidence says yes (economyAudit.storageCaps.test.ts asserts them, decisionRegistry.ts:103-111 lists storageMax/foodSpoilageRate as the daily production owner's writes, OWNERSHIP_OVERVIEW.md:16 names `updateStorageCaps` as the caps owner, building descriptions promise +300/+800 wood, and frontierCombat.ts:669 assumes storageMax.gold is 'effectively unlimited'). If instead the static worldGen caps (gold 2000) are now the design, then updateStorageCaps and its test are stale and should be deleted — but then the Barn/Silo/WoodStorehouse/Warehouse cost is currently pure waste and the descriptions are wrong. One of the two must change.
- No test covers hunting/fishing/greenhouse ledger amounts, so findings 2-3 can be fixed without touching locked-in behaviour (weatherConsequences.test.ts only pins the farm path via `added`).
- Determinism: hunting success/foughtBack use Math.random() in a daily sim path, but that matches the established project convention (Math.random is used for wolf recruitment in tickLayerSystems.ts:104, rival camp dice, etc.; simRng's seeded global override is only installed by enableSeededGlobalRandom and the deterministicSeed test relies on setSimSeed + worldGen). Reporting it here would be noise, so it is not a finding in this group.
- Not verified by execution: this was a read-only static audit; no tests or typecheck were run (permission scope is read-only and the task forbids modification).

### A13-ecology

Confirmed findings from this group: 5

Files read: `src/game/ecologyStage.ts`, `src/game/ecosystemPressure.ts`, `src/game/dailyEcology.ts`, `src/game/dailyGrassEcology.ts`, `src/game/grassEcology.ts`, `src/game/ecoBreakdown.ts`, `src/game/temperature.ts`, `src/game/terrainSystems.ts`, `src/game/speciesConfig.ts`, `src/game/animalCare.ts`, `src/game/gameTick.ts`, `src/game/tickLayerDaily.ts`, `src/game/dailyWorldEvents.ts`, `src/game/tickLayerSystems.ts`, `src/game/deerParliament.ts`, `src/game/storyEvents.ts`, `src/game/simQueries.ts`, `src/game/simulation/simulationEntities.ts`, `src/game/entityCounts.ts`, `src/game/gameConstants.ts`, `src/game/dayCycleClock.ts`, `src/game/entityFactory.ts`, `src/game/simRng.ts`, `src/game/simHelpers.ts`, `src/game/saveLoad.ts`, `src/game/simWorker/simPrep.ts`, `src/game/simBuffers/simDelta.ts`, `src/components/tabPanels/NatureTabPanel.tsx`, `tests/animalCare.test.ts`, `tests/deerParliament.test.ts`, `tests/gameTick.layerOrder.test.ts`, `tests/deterministicSeed.test.ts`, `tests/fullYear.integration.test.ts`

PER-FILE VERDICT
- src/game/ecologyStage.ts — Sole owner of the valley-stage ladder, correctly parked behind `ValleyEcology.ENABLED=false` and driven only from the daily layer (dailyWorldEvents.ts:149, after tickEcosystemMetrics at :146); stage/streak math and the ±1 transition model are consistent with getAbsoluteCalendarDay, but the notify cooldown, the `current !== 'damaged'` collapse cap and the >=2.0 ratio tier are dead code (findings 4-6).
- src/game/ecosystemPressure.ts — Pure read-only report; demand/recovery units (per-day bite-energy, 8-energy bite, TICKS_PER_DAY scaling) match dailyGrassEcology's batch growth and tickLayerSystems' grazing bite; the only defect is the deer-only override that can mask rabbit/wildkin overgrazing (finding 2).
- src/game/dailyEcology.ts — Correct daily owner of pollution/ecosystemHealth/biodiversityIndex (all carried by saveSchema, simPrep and simDelta); its unconditional recompute is what erases event-applied eco deltas (finding 3) and it is the reference side of the ecoBreakdown drift (finding 1).
- src/game/dailyGrassEcology.ts — Healthy: growth is scaled once by TICKS_PER_DAY, the per-day reproduction chance is the correct `1-(1-p)^72` transform of the configured per-tick chance, and pushNewEntity→recordGrassBirth / markGrassDead→recordGrassDeath keep the cached grass-population snapshot (and therefore the cap) accurate inside the same loop; `Math.random()` here is the globally seeded stream installed by worldGen (`setSimSeed` + `enableSeededGlobalRandom`), so it is deterministic per run seed. `grass.flash` is decayed by 1 per day although nothing ever sets it and every other flash decays per tick — harmless today, worth one look if a graze-flash is ever added.
- src/game/grassEcology.ts — Constants verified against SPECIES_CONFIG (Grass maxEnergy 100 = GRASS_MAX_ENERGY, thresholds/chances match) and against the systems-layer bite (GRAZE_BITE_ENERGY 8, graze min 5); `GRAZER_METABOLISM` is a hand-mirrored copy of SPECIES_CONFIG grazer values (currently identical) so a SPECIES_CONFIG tune would silently desync the pressure math; `getGrazerDailyDemand` is dead (finding 7).
- src/game/ecoBreakdown.ts — Presentational, but it re-derives a sim decision and has drifted from its owner (finding 1); it also omits the owner's `Math.max(0, …)` on pollutionLevel (unreachable today because raw pollution is non-negative).
- src/game/temperature.ts — No findings: deterministic pure function (sin-hash over year/day), clamps nothing but needs nothing (weather/season lookups are exhaustive and `Math.floor` handles negative noise); note it hardcodes 360 where DAYS_PER_YEAR exists, and an unknown weather/season would make the result NaN.
- src/game/terrainSystems.ts — `isInsideCompletedBuilding` / `isValidHumanSpawnPosition` / `getTerrainEfficiencyMultiplier` / `getAdjacencyMultiplier` are correct for the edge cases I checked (null worldMap, negative/out-of-range tile indices via `tiles[ty]?.[tx]`, buildings with x,y as top-left per buildingRotation.isEntityOnBuilding). Two low-value notes: `getTileAt` hardcodes `/ 10` instead of the canonical `TERRAIN_TILE_SIZE` used by every other module, and `findHumanSpawnNear`'s last-resort fallback returns the unvalidated map centre (dailyPopulation.ts:79 spawns a settler there when all 16 probes fail), so the function's name promises a valid spawn that the fallback does not guarantee.
- src/game/speciesConfig.ts — Loads correctly; the legacy `cd(hours)` conversion (×TICKS_PER_HOUR) matches the step-based cooldown decrement in tickLayerSystems (`cooldown -= step`, step = LAYER_SYSTEMS_INTERVAL = 4), and `energyGain` keys match EntityType values. Unresolved: Wolf `energyGain.rabbit = 30` versus Fox `energyGain.rabbit = 450` (tickLayerSystems.ts:458-462 applies the raw value on a kill) means a wolf must eat ~4.4 rabbits/day against a 900 max energy — possibly a typo for 300, but I found no test or comment establishing intent, so I did not file it.
- src/game/animalCare.ts — Matches its spec and animalCare.test.ts: daily pulse, affordability check before spending, colony-day flag prevents a double charge, warning→shortage escalation and recovery reset behave as the test locks in; food is spent after production (tickDailyWorldEvents runs at the end of tickLayerDaily). Note: `tickAnimalCare` returns early when `tamed === 0`, leaving a stale 'shortage' status (no UI consumer today), and the same colony-day flag means the systems layer can see `tamedAnimalsFedToday() === false` on the day-boundary tick before the daily layer feeds.

UNRESOLVED / DESIGN QUESTIONS
1. Should `state.ecosystemHealth` be a purely derived metric (current behaviour, which makes every story/election/wolf-choice eco delta temporary) or a persistent value with modifiers? Findings 1 and 3 both stem from this — the decision needs one owner and a test.
2. Is the ValleyEcology ladder's re-enable path expected to be exercised? I audited it as live code, but all three ecologyStage defects (findings 4-6) are currently inert because `ENABLED=false`.
3. Wolf rabbit energy gain (30) vs Fox (450): typo or deliberate deer-only wolf economy?
4. `findHumanSpawnNear` fallback and `grass.flash` per-day decay (above) — not filed as findings because I cannot show a current observable failure.

VERIFICATION: read-only audit; no files were created, modified or deleted. Findings were re-checked against the exact lines and against the call sites listed in filesReviewed; the only existing tests touching this group (tests/animalCare.test.ts, tests/deerParliament.test.ts, tests/gameTick.layerOrder.test.ts, tests/deterministicSeed.test.ts, tests/fullYear.integration.test.ts) do not lock in any behaviour I report as a defect.

### A14-worldgen-terrain

Confirmed findings from this group: 8

Files read: `src/game/worldGen.ts`, `src/game/terrainGen.ts`, `src/game/migration.ts`, `src/game/blueberryForaging.ts`

METHOD / VERIFICATION: read all four files in full, plus every caller (dailyWorldEvents.ts, tickLayerDaily.ts, dailyBuildingEconomy.ts, humanHuntingBehavior.ts, gameTick.ts, entityCounts.ts, simPrep.ts, simDelta.ts, saveSchema.ts, entityIndex.ts, entityCatalog.ts, simInvariants.ts) and the locked tests (tests/blueberryForaging.test.ts, tests/migration.herds.test.ts, tests/terrainGen.riverGeneration.test.ts, tests/deterministicSeed.test.ts). Three read-only empirical probes were run with the repo's local tsx (node node_modules/tsx/dist/cli.mjs -e "..."), no files were created or modified.

PER-FILE VERDICT
- src/game/worldGen.ts — WORTH FIXING: two always-true/unreachable guards in replenishDepletedWildlife (findings 2 and 8) and the empty entityByType index left by initGame (finding 3). Rest of the module (blueberry spawn cap, ring/random spawners, founding couple, immigrant factory) checks out.
- src/game/terrainGen.ts — MOSTLY SOUND: deterministic, tested river spine; three low-severity dead/guard gaps (findings 4, 6, 7); no cadence or authority issue (pure one-shot initialization, no sim state ownership).
- src/game/migration.ts — ONE REAL DEFECT: herd spawns bypass the terrain passability rule used by every other spawner (finding 1, measured); the departure/counting/clamping logic otherwise matches tests/migration.herds.test.ts; other item is message/accounting wording (finding 5).
- src/game/blueberryForaging.ts — CLEAN, no findings. Single owner, correct daily regrowth cadence (called once per day from tickDailyBuildingEconomy.ts:252 via the tick%72 daily layer), yield clamped 0..6, addResource returns the actual added amount so the floating text and the storage-full abort are correct, search is staggered and grid-based.

UNRESOLVED / DESIGN QUESTIONS
1. SIMULATION_AUTHORITY §5 blueberry clause 'An invalid, dead, depleted, or out-of-range target is cleared': blueberryForaging.ts:85-91 validates alive/kind/yield but never distance, so a held target is not re-checked against BLUEBERRY_SEARCH_RADIUS. I could not construct a reachable violation (distance only shrinks while a target is held, and every ineligible tick clears it), so it is not filed as a finding — but the invariant clause has no implementation.
2. migrationNextHerdSize only ever decreases (migration.ts:84 clamps to [4,16] but never restores). The file header promises 'let them pass and next autumn brings them back, fat as ever', which holds only if the player never takes a single deer, ever. The shrink is locked by tests/migration.herds.test.ts; recovery is not specified anywhere I found. Needs a design decision (intentional permanent memory vs. slow recovery).
3. worldGen.initGame creates an already-pregnant immigrant wife (worldGen.ts:517-520) without humanRelationships — the declared sole conception owner. It is whole-settler construction rather than conception for an existing settler and entityFactory sets pregnancyDueProgress, so no §5 pregnancy invariant breaks; flagged only because the ownership table says 'New conception: humanRelationships.ts only'.
4. worldGen.initGame ignores InitGameOptions.width/height when generating the map (line 687 passes `size`), so a caller supplying custom dimensions gets state.width/height that disagree with worldMap.width/height (and terrainGen.ts:674-676 explicitly fixed the mirror-image case). Latent only: no production or test caller passes width/height today.
5. terrainGen.seededRandom (lines 6-12) only coerces seed === 0; a non-zero seed that is a multiple of 2147483647 (e.g. 2147483647, 4294967294) yields s = 0 for the whole run, so rng() returns the constant (0-1)/2147483646, i.e. a negative value that breaks the documented [0,1) contract and makes Math.floor(rng()*4) === -1 (topPeaks becomes 4) and `rng() < 0.5` always true. Not filed as a finding because production seeds come from Math.floor(Math.random()*1_000_000) (worldGen.ts:568) or a saved map seed, so the degenerate value is unreachable in game; recorded because generateWorldMap is exported and takes an arbitrary seed.
6. worldGen naming determinism: the founder/immigrant name pools come from an asynchronous census load while the PRNG stream is seeded (nameLoader.pickFrom uses Math.random, which enableSeededGlobalRandom makes seeded). App.tsx awaits loadNames() before initGame and then calls fixDefaultNames, but a headless caller that does not await loadNames() can name the same seed from the 15-name fallback pool. Not a defect in worldGen itself.
7. Minor, no behavioural claim: replenishDepletedWildlife assigns state.wildlifeCounts from computeWildlifeCounts(state.entities) at line 460 although the animals it just spawned live in ctx.newEntities, so that value undercounts and is overwritten by gameTick:268 in the same tick; and terrainGen hardcodes 10 in isFootprintBuildable/forEachTileInRadius (lines 138-141, 163-164) while using TERRAIN_TILE_SIZE elsewhere (they agree today, TERRAIN_TILE_SIZE = 10).

### A15-daily-events-population

Confirmed findings from this group: 9

Files read: `src/game/dailyWorldEvents.ts`, `src/game/worldEvents.ts`, `src/game/storyEvents.ts`, `src/game/storyHelpers.ts`, `src/game/challenges.ts`, `src/game/dailyChallenges.ts`, `src/game/dailyPopulation.ts`, `src/game/populationGrowth.ts`, `src/game/famineDesperation.ts`, `src/game/valleyChronicle.ts`

Per-file verdicts: dailyWorldEvents.ts - layer order and daily cadence are correct (fixed call list, per-3-day wildlife refill, day-gated yearly/mid-year/first-week events, election buildup only at year rollover, skill decay behind isNewCalendarDayTick); only the seasonal-festival guarantee is wrong (reported). worldEvents.ts - cadence correct (weather re-rolls every 50 systems pulses = 200 ticks ~2.8 days per the comment; disaster gate isProductionTick every 40 days at tick%72==0, progress counted in systems pulses); fire/flood/tornado/plague use the stored radius and kill through killHuman/killedThisTick; only the earthquake branch omits the radius/faction filter (reported). storyEvents.ts - every substantive guard/effect matches tests/storyEvents.test.ts; the year-0 welcome gate is unreachable (reported); the children-shelter feed constant is 10*HUMAN_DAILY_FOOD_CONSUMPTION = 30/day, matching the test, but the doc comment on line 44 still says '2 food/day each' (stale comment only); resolveChildrenShelter returns true for any unknown choiceId (line 315), silently consuming the card with no flags while every sibling resolver maps unknown choices to a defined default - noted, not reported. storyHelpers.ts - seededRoll/eligibleDayForStory are pure and hashSalt-keyed (used by 5 authored-story modules), AUTHORED_STORY_COOLDOWN_FLAG has real consumers; the reputation clamp is the finding (reported); pushStoryCard is a byte-identical second implementation of storyEvents.offerStoryEvent (both dedupe by id into pendingStoryEvents) - maintainability duplication, not reported. challenges.ts - catalog, completion switch and progress are consistent with the description texts; iron is present in every reward object and rewardText but never granted (reported against dailyChallenges); getChallengeProgress returns null for growing_village/great_city/century so those three show no progress bar in ChallengesPanel (missing feature, deliberately not reported). dailyChallenges.ts - rewards are granted once because the completed flag is persisted (saveSchema/simPrep/simDelta all carry challenges); besides iron and the ledger mismatch, line 15's `{ ...state, ecoHealthYearsAbove80: state.ecoHealthYearsAbove80 }` is a no-op copy (the spread already copies that field and isChallengeComplete only reads year/ecoHealthYearsAbove80/unlockedTechs/tradeRoutes) - style-adjacent, not reported. dailyPopulation.ts - backwards dead-prune, pruneFactionWanderStates id list, cap/immigration-chance math (clamped to 0.95) and residence assignment through the dayCycle owner all look correct; only the unreachable couple-split repair (reported). populationGrowth.ts - report/cap arithmetic and getOpenPlayerBeds (leader-house aware) are correct; the snapshot cache staleness is reported. famineDesperation.ts - daily cadence, domain-isolated seeded stream, non-lethal by construction (no energy/death writes) and the victim selection are correct; only the unreachable self-bite guard (reported). valleyChronicle.ts - chapters unlock once from the daily layer via the persisted chronicleChapters list, rewards via addCappedResource are capped like every other grant; the delta/prep omission is reported. Minor observations not reported: tickElectionPromises is invoked twice per day (tickLayerDaily.ts:62 and dailyWorldEvents.ts:131, plus tickLeaderPromise which delegates to it) but is idempotent through the evaluatedKey flag; challenge and children-shelter food writes bypass recordFoodConsumed so the economy ledger's 'consumed' side is incomplete (children: storyEvents.ts:102); the population cache key also cannot see residence type/faction changes. Open design questions: is the map-wide earthquake intentional, and is '20 guaranteed festival days' a hard requirement or only copy?

VERDICT — src/game/groupEvents.ts: a structurally sound, well-ordered frontier/requests owner; I found no reachable invariant, cadence, determinism, numeric or double-spend error, only the accept-rule duplication (medium) and five low-severity robustness/consistency issues above. Verified-clean areas I specifically checked: (1) cadence — tickVillageRequests/tickVisitorGroups/tickWorldRivalSettlements/rollYearlyWorldEvent/tryMidYearVisitorEvent/tryFirstWeekVisitor are reached only from tickDailyWorldEvents (tickLayerDaily runs only when tick % TICKS_PER_DAY === 0) and every command/eligibility entry delegates from simWorker/commands.ts, so no decision is moved between cadences; `isNewCalendarDayTick(state)` is always true on those daily ticks (markCalendarDayProcessed, dayCycle.ts:320-324), so the visitor gift/daysLeft gate never silently skips a day and the ungated departure check at line 573 keeps departures working even on a duplicate tick; (2) village-request invariants — one active request, id `vreq_caravan_provisions_<groupId>_<day>`, bounded expiry (day > expiresDay), repeated/stale/unaffordable/storage-blocked accepts all leave request + resources untouched (lines 157-188), and activeVillageRequest/villageRequestCooldownUntilDay/villageRequestHistory are carried by save (saveSchema.ts:16 + saveLoad spread), simPrep (142-144/226-228) and simDelta (295-297/436-438); (3) resource economy — every spend is preceded by the matching affordability check, addCappedResource/applyTradeCost cannot go negative, the purse arithmetic in tradeWithVisitors conserves gold in both directions and no path both pays and receives gold; (4) command-path safety — every exporting mutator clones first (cloneWorldStateForAction), so nothing writes the caller's world; eligibility helpers are pure; (5) refugee admission — clearing faction/groupId, JobType.Settler, reproductionCooldown = 0, finalizeSettlerAge, then assignMissingResidences + syncResidenceOccupants keeps the workforce/residence invariants true, and hotel/factionWander residue self-heals (pruneHotelGuests drops non-visitors, pruneFactionWanderStates drops the dead).

### A17-combat

Confirmed findings from this group: 6

Files read: `src/game/frontierCombat.ts`, `src/game/combat.ts`, `src/game/defenseStructures.ts`, `src/game/watchtowerDetection.ts`, `src/game/militiaBalance.ts`, `src/game/combatTech.ts`

VERDICTS
- frontierCombat.ts - Sound overall: one owner for raid queue (maybeQueueRaid) and resolution (respondTo*/launchRaidOnRival), idempotent command resolvers (unknown/duplicate event id returns the clone untouched), ratio thresholds (1.35/0.95/0.65 and 1.35/1.0) and the *_HINT / *_LABELS strings match the code, and loot is clamped to available stock in applyRaidLootTaken. Defects: grantRaidSpoils negative headroom (finding 2) and the barricade eligibility gap (finding 4).
- combat.ts - Pure readers, no side effects, no state ownership. One high-severity defect (finding 1); everything else (forge/blacksmith gates, predator type set, armament-step table) is consistent with forge.ts and the research table.
- defenseStructures.ts - Wall/tower/guard math is correct and caps rival buildings out via countCompletedDefenseBuildings; MILITIA_BALANCE is a genuine single source of truth. Only finding 5.
- watchtowerDetection.ts - Correct daily cadence (called once from tickDailyWorldEvents:134), completed towers only, marching bands only, radius respected when detecting, and hiddenFromPlayer is recomputed each tick from detectedByPatrol (humanTick.ts:472) so the reveal persists as intended. No findings.
- militiaBalance.ts - Correct tier-replacement maths for weapons/armour, and structures feed only barricadeStrength. Findings 3 and 6.
- combatTech.ts - Pure constants; all nine ids exist in createInitialResearchNodes (gameTypes.ts:922-931) and match the forge orders' techId. No findings.

UNRESOLVED / DESIGN QUESTIONS
1. Chivalrous intent (finding 3): the code adds one flat 8% while the emitted label says N x 8%. The comment ('Chivalrous settlers add a protective edge to the whole militia') does not settle which side is authoritative; the fix must pick one. The same decision determines whether the missing isPlayerHuman filter is a bonus bug or only a display bug.
2. watchtowerDetection.ts:34-41 clears hiddenFromPlayer / sets detectedByPatrol for EVERY rival of a detected group, even members far outside the 260px radius. humanPatrolBehavior.ts:21-29 does exactly the same for patrols, and all of a rival camp's humans share groupId = rival.id, so I treated whole-war-band reveal as intended and did not file it - worth confirming the intended unit of detection is the band, not the individual.
3. Determinism: maybeQueueRaid rolls its raid chance with Math.random() (frontierCombat.ts:834) inside the daily tick, and rollRivalOutgoingRaidResponse's rng parameter (line 726) is never injected by its only caller (line 1339). Seeded getSimRng streams exist (entityFactory, migration, famineDesperation, prisonGuardDuty), but Math.random is pervasive in the same layer (rivalEvents.ts:228-267, tickLayerSystems.ts) and tests/deterministicSeed.test.ts replays only 100 ticks with no rival action, so no test locks this either way.
4. Latent, not currently reachable: researchedEffect sets found = true on a target match even when the requested field is absent (combat.ts:45), so an add-mode caller gets 0 instead of undefined, and getCounterAttackChance's 'add !== undefined' branch would then return that 0, suppressing the 0.45/0.55 default. No current research node has a counter_attack effect without add.
5. UI duplication (outside my assigned files, noted only): App.tsx:1410-1424 restates all three raid gates inline instead of calling getRaidChoiceEligibility - the helper added precisely so no caller restates the owner's rules. After the finding-4 fix, the UI's disabled state would drift from the owner's decision.

OTHER UNUSED SURFACE (not filed, no correctness claim): countArmedMilitia (frontierCombat.ts:193-197), getDefenseStructureBreakdown (defenseStructures.ts:151-173, which duplicates the militiaBalance breakdown lines), the two @deprecated *FromBreakdown helpers (militiaBalance.ts:236-244), and OutgoingRaidEvent.marchFoodCost, written at frontierCombat.ts:1361 but never read.

VERDICTS: (1) src/game/moonHowler.ts — lifecycle logic is otherwise coherent and matches SIMULATION_AUTHORITY §5: the `activeCursed === 0` gate blocks a second curse, survivor return works via syncMoonHowlerForms every tick, revert honours prison/job/residence caps, dead entities never re-enter occupants (line 446 guard), the 2-hour cooldown is correctly multiplied by TICKS_PER_HOUR (line 675), and the 'inside a building' sweep keeps the hunt outdoors. 3 findings (high/medium/low). (2) src/game/deerParliament.ts — offer gate, eligible day, one-card-at-a-time check, affordability re-check and the keep-the-card contract are all correct and test-covered; only the ecology effects are dead (1 medium finding). (3) src/game/renffrStar.ts — NO FINDINGS. The omen roll is a pure function of world seed + tick, omen/chatter state is persisted in saveSchema, simPrep and simDelta, the phase/life counters are consistent, and drawRenffrOmen is presentational (streakT saturating at 1 after ~36 of the 50 phase-0 ticks is a visual hold only, not reported). (4) src/game/factionWander.ts — 1 low finding; the wander map is pruned daily (dailyPopulation.ts:151) and cleared on new game/load, movement math cannot divide by zero (`dist >= arrive` before the normalisation at line 412), camp sites are clamped to an 80px margin (groupEvents.pickSite) so wander targets stay on-map, and the per-entity Map iteration in pruneFactionWanderStates is delete-safe.

UNRESOLVED / NEEDS A DESIGN DECISION: (a) EK-C6 realtime reconciliation — tickMoonHowlerCycle calls assignMissingResidences/assignMissingWorkers from the realtime layer (moonHowler.ts:971-979 and 1055-1059). The §3 ownership table lists housing/workforce reconciliation as tickLayerAssign plus immediate place/recruit/death/divorce/arrest, and the moonHowler owner row allows only 'curse, transformation, return, cure, replacement event'; the EK-C6 comment justifies it but the authority document was not updated. No test covers it. Decide whether to document the exception or delegate the sync, rather than changing it silently. (b) revertToHumanForm's restore branches (moonHowler.ts:495-523) validate only `completed && faction !== 'rival'`, not the building's role: a stale homeBuildingId/residenceBuildingId pointing at a building of the wrong role would push the settler into that building's occupants with a mismatched field (invariant errors 'is not a completed workplace' / 'is not a residence'). I could not construct a live path to such an id (all residence writers validate, building ids are monotonic), so it is not reported, but the missing isResidenceBuilding/BUILDING_JOB_TYPES guard is a real robustness gap. (c) migrateLegacyMoonHowler (lines 849-867) marks every legacy permanent werewolf cursed, so a legacy save containing 2+ werewolves would load with multiple living cursed Howlers (invariant check at simulationInvariants.ts:246-268); no path was found that ever created more than one, so not reported. (d) moonHowlerSaved.prisonerUntilTick is not tick-scaled by migrateTickTimeline while world.tick is (saveLoad.ts:247-250), so a save taken mid-hunt by a prisoner gets a sentence deadline in the old 24-tick/day scale; one-shot legacy-migration skew only. (e) factionWander writes `entity.chatTicks = 18 + ...` directly (lines 312, 341) while every other chat writer goes through sayHumanChatPhrase -> activeChatTicksFromLegacyDuration (legacy ticks / 3), so visitor bubbles are ~1/3 the intended length; the VisitPhase member 'wander_edge' and idleTicksFor's 'return_camp' case are never constructed. Cosmetic/dead code, not reported. (f) decisionRegistry.ts:209 says the Moon Howler writes title 'Moonslayer' but the code grants 'Howlerbane' (moonHowler.ts:757) — registry drift, not a code defect. (g) spawnMoonHowlerDebug does not check the one-Howler limit (canMoonHowlerCurse has no activeCursed check), so the debug button can create a second cursed Howler; debug-only, not reported. METHOD: grading-relevant tests read directly (tests/*.test.ts is excluded from the grep tool here), call sites verified for every reported item, and every finding re-read against the exact lines quoted.

### A19-leadership

Confirmed findings from this group: 6

Files read: `src/game/villageLeadership.ts`, `src/game/leaderHouse.ts`, `src/game/townHall.ts`, `src/game/electionVotes.ts`, `src/game/electionPromises.ts`, `src/game/weddingDiplomacy.ts`, `src/game/gameTick.ts`, `src/game/tickLayerDaily.ts`, `src/game/tickLayerRealtime.ts`, `src/game/dailyWorldEvents.ts`, `src/game/dailyBuildingEconomy.ts`, `src/game/humanTick.ts`, `src/game/humanVenueBehavior.ts`, `src/game/residencyReconciliation.ts`, `src/game/residencySelection.ts`, `src/game/residencyOccupancy.ts`, `src/game/householdComposition.ts`, `src/game/dayCycleClock.ts`, `src/game/humanSchedule.ts`, `src/game/moonHowler.ts`, `src/game/storyHelpers.ts`, `src/game/storyEvents.ts`, `src/game/eventLog.ts`, `src/game/simHelpers.ts`, `src/game/workforce.ts`, `src/game/playerHuman.ts`, `src/game/saveLoad.ts`, `src/game/simulation/decisionRegistry.ts`, `src/game/simulation/simulationInvariants.ts`, `tests/electionVotes.test.ts`, `tests/villageLeadership.actingHead.test.ts`, `tests/villageLeadership.titlePoints.test.ts`, `tests/villageLeadership.legacyElectionToken.test.ts`, `tests/electionGossip.dedup.test.ts`, `tests/leaderHouse.workforce.test.ts`, `tests/leaderRemarriage.residency.test.ts`, `tests/festival.behavior.test.ts`, `tests/virtualPlayer.test.ts`

Verdicts: villageLeadership.ts - sound overall (ceremony phases, ranking, save-token migration and status text all check out), but two scheduling defects (vacancy delay not honoured; no re-arm after an empty-candidate vacancy) and one festival-slot clobber. leaderHouse.ts - matches the 2026-08-20 workforce decision and the existing tests; one cross-module staleness with the Moon Howler occupation snapshot. townHall.ts - perk/festival helpers are consistent and gated, but the civic-petition rate limit is not implemented (findings 1). electionVotes.ts - correct; deterministic merit/bond/tie-break behaviour matches tests/electionVotes.test.ts. electionPromises.ts - deterministic (seeded tie-break), idempotent via the evaluated flag, save-carried through storyFlags; no correctness defect found. weddingDiplomacy.ts - seeded, staged, affordability-checked chain resolves correctly; only the over-restrictive production gate and a status-blind eligibility mirror.\n\nUnresolved / design questions:\n1. All authored stories share one flaw: storyEvents.tickPendingStoryEvents (storyEvents.ts:155-160) silently drops an expired card, while weddingDiplomacy stamps FLAG_OFFERED at offer time (line 101) and only pushes stage 2 when FLAG_STATUS is gift_sent/declined (line 258). An unanswered stage-1 card therefore consumes the one-time story slot forever, and an unanswered stage-2 card leaves FLAG_STATUS = gift_sent permanently. This is systemic across the authored-story family, so I did not raise it as a group finding; it needs a product decision (resolve-with-default on expiry vs. re-offer).\n2. getWeddingDiplomacyChoiceEligibility is status-blind: it gates choice ids by resource without checking FLAG_STATUS, while resolveWeddingDiplomacy routes by status, so it can report ok:false for an answer the resolver would accept (stage-1 id while in stage 2) and ok:true for a stage-2 id that resolveStage1 would treat as 'decline'. Only reachable through a consumer that sends off-card ids (the bot uses card choices), so I left it out; a `storyFlag(state, FLAG_STATUS)` switch would make the mirrored contract exact.\n3. Dead/unused API in this group: getActiveElectionPromises and PromiseDetail-style progress (electionPromises.ts:161) and isElectionCeremonyActive (villageLeadership.ts:418) have no consumers anywhere in src/ or tests/, and setNewLeaderPromise (villageLeadership.ts:1026) is an unreferenced legacy hook - the promise progress is never shown to the player even though promises are recorded and evaluated. Also electionPromisesForYear is only used internally.\n4. tickElectionPromises is invoked three times per colony day (tickLayerDaily.ts:62, dailyWorldEvents.ts:100 via tickLeaderPromise, dailyWorldEvents.ts:131). It is idempotent (the evaluated flag short-circuits), so this is redundant rather than wrong, but the triple wiring is easy to get out of sync.\n5. Election gossip runs from the realtime layer on `state.tick % 18 / % 24` (villageLeadership.ts:770-775) and each roll scans the whole population (rankLeadershipCandidates -> getLeadershipScoreBreakdown) plus two full eventLog filters (assessVillageHealth, countLeaderScandalsDuringTerm; eventLog is capped at 2000). tests/electionGossip.dedup.test.ts deliberately pins the daily-layer skip during a ceremony, so I did not raise it as a cadence violation, but a 60-day gossip phase driven by a realtime full-population scan plus unseeded Math.random is worth a design look.\n6. canHostTownFestival (townHall.ts:104-127) omits the `faction !== 'rival'` test every other townHall helper has. I could not make it reachable: rival settlements are only built with House/Farm/Well (groupEvents.ts:447-456) and Watchtower/Market (rivalEvents.ts:86-115), never a TownHall, and player buildings leave faction undefined. Not reported, but the missing guard would be the only validation between a typed command and a rival-owned building if a TownHall ever became a rival building.\n7. ELECTION_PARTY_DAYS = 1 combined with the day-boundary decrement means the revelry actually lasts ~47 ticks (the reveal lands at tick % 72 === 25 for a rollover-started ceremony), not a full day; cosmetic, not reported.\n8. tickElectionPromises logs broken promises as a 'scandal' event carrying the leader's name (electionPromises.ts:230-237). That deliberately feeds countLeaderScandalsDuringTerm (-5 record points at the next election) but also increments the dashboard/rumour-ledger scandal counters (dashboardData.ts:325, rumourLedger.ts:95) that were written for affair scandals; looks intentional, flagged for confirmation.\n9. docs/private/archive/election-ceremony-v0.5.md still describes 10-year terms, a 2-year vacancy delay, one-day gossip and a 3-day party; the code has deliberately moved on (2-year terms, 0.25-year vacancy, 60-day gossip, 1-day party) with explanatory comments, so I treated the code as authoritative.\n10. tickLeaderVacancy clears villageLeaderId without calling applyLeaderOccupation (line 905), unlike appointFoundingLeader (467) and runVillageElection (811). I could not build a reachable path to a living, in-state, non-acting incumbent (only moonHowler sets EntityType.Werewolf and it always sets moonHowlerCursed, and faction is never set on a settler), so I folded the practical consequence into finding 4 instead of reporting it separately.

### A2-types-constants

Confirmed findings from this group: 6

Files read: `src/game/gameTypes.ts`, `src/game/gameConstants.ts`, `src/game/dayCycleConstants.ts`, `src/game/ecologyTypes.ts`, `src/game/raidUtils.ts`, `src/game/entityCatalog.ts`, `src/game/playerHuman.ts`, `src/game/stats.ts`, `src/game/version.ts`, `src/game/simulation/simulationTypes.ts`, `src/game/entityCounts.ts`, `src/game/entityIndex.ts`, `src/game/entityTypeCache.ts`, `src/game/nodeRuntime.ts`, `src/game/saveSchema.ts`, `src/game/mapBounds.ts`

Per-file verdicts (all 16 files read in full):
- gameTypes.ts — mostly declarative data; two live defects reported (drought_resist has no consumer; hunt_yield/hunt_food key mismatch on the Hunting-Spot line). All research node ids, prerequisite ids, BUILDING_JOB_TYPES/JOB_LABELS coverage, unlockRequirement ids and forge techIds resolve correctly. `WorldState.leaderPromise` (line 745) and the `LeaderPromise` interface are written/read nowhere in the repo (dead declaration for a feature that looks half-removed). `snapToGrid` (line 995) would produce NaN for gridSize 0, but both call paths pass GRID_SIZE (20) or a clamp of it, so it is unreachable. `PopulationHistoryPoint` (line 492) is an unused alias.
- gameConstants.ts — no finding. Checked each value against its documented derivation and its consumer: TICKS_PER_DAY=72/DAYS_PER_YEAR=360/DAYS_PER_SEASON=90, Human meal cadence + MEAL_ENERGY_RESTORE (humanNeeds.ts), Relationship factors (humanRelationships.ts:1404 matches the documented church floor formula; 16+floor(rand*12)=16..27 matches the comment), Prison 24h/8h/0.05 (prisonGuardDuty.ts:50-57), FAMINE/Social/FEMALE_CLASS_WEIGHTS (10 entries matches HUMAN_FEMALE_CLASS_COUNT=10; note the legacy female sheet array only has 8 entries, so classes 8-9 fall back to the base sprite if USE_NEW_SETTLER_ART_PREVIEW is ever set false) and VirtualPlayer thresholds (virtualPlayer.ts usages match the documented semantics).
- dayCycleConstants.ts — no finding. isNightHour / isFullMoonDay / isFullMoonNight correctly implement the 20:00→06:00 full-moon night including the pre-dawn `colonyDay - 1` branch; the day value used by callers is the absolute colony day.
- ecologyTypes.ts — one-line union type, nothing executable; no finding.
- raidUtils.ts — no finding. The typeof guards do what the name promises (missing/null/undefined -> 'deadline unknown'); only non-finite numbers would slip through as "NaN days left", and I found no producer of a non-finite createdAtTick/expiresAtTick (saveLoad repairs both fields).
- entityCatalog.ts — no finding. ensureAliveIndex collects stale ids before deleting them (no mutate-while-iterating), getAlive/getAliveByType/getEntityByType return copies, get() hides dead entities. `applyTickDelta` (lines 69-104) is never called anywhere — gameLoop always calls `catalog.rebuild(this.world.entities)` per tick result — so the documented "Worker path syncs from tick delta" does not happen; dead method, no correctness impact.
- playerHuman.ts — no finding. Predicate matches its doc; an unknown/misspelled `faction` string counts as a player settler by design (explicitly documented in the file).
- stats.ts — three findings (deaths always 0, upgrades delta/level mismatch, born-window regression). The marriage heuristic (line 74) is an acknowledged approximation and was not reported.
- version.ts — no finding; GAME_VERSION is the save gate in parseSaveJson and matches the docs.
- simulation/simulationTypes.ts — type-only TickContext; optional scratch fields (residenceOccupants, grassPopulation, wildlifePopulation, grassCap) are lazily filled by the layers that use them (humanTick.ts:230, tickLayerSystems.ts:175-188, dailyGrassEcology.ts:18-22); no finding.
- entityCounts.ts — no finding. Buckets map 1:1 to WildlifeCounts keys, human/wildlife split matches gameTick's census. A `trade_caravan` human is counted in none of humans/visitorHumans/rivalHumans, but the only consumer of those two transient counts (formatPopulationBrief) has no callers outside the barrel, so there is no impact.
- entityIndex.ts — no finding. The canonical WeakMap marker correctly forces a rebuild for cloned/transferred maps; `state.entityById` is assigned nowhere else (grep), and unindexLivingEntity's `alive = false` side effect is intended by its only caller (worldEvents.ts:52-54).
- entityTypeCache.ts — no finding found, but the design is worth stating: the cache key is the WorldState identity and the value is a bucket array that must match the live entity composition. I verified the composition-change guards at gameTick.ts:250-263 (deaths/untracked spawns/newEntities/daily) and that every `entity.type` mutation goes through moonHowler rebuild paths or a cloned world (settlerInteractionActions.ts:117), and that command paths that add entities return a structuredClone (recruitSettler), which changes world identity and misses the cache. `getCachedEntityByType` ignores its `aliveEntities` argument on a hit and does not populate the cache (cacheEntityByType does), which is intentional per the module doc.
- nodeRuntime.ts — no finding. Node-only dynamic imports are guarded by isNodeRuntime() and the whole read is wrapped so browser callers get null.
- saveSchema.ts — one finding (allow-list omissions). Additionally, `migrateEntityPersistedFields` re-applies ENTITY_PERSISTED_FIELDS after loadGameFromParsed already spread `...e`, so that loop is a no-op for a normal JSON round-trip (only its ensureEntitySkills call has an effect) — dead safety net, not a bug.
- mapBounds.ts — no finding. The clamp is correct and inclusive for finite input; NaN is a silent no-op (NaN < 0 and NaN > width are both false), and I deliberately did not report it as numeric-safety because every direction normalization I inspected guards with `|| 1` (humanTick.ts:395-1371) and JSON cannot carry NaN, so I could not point at a producer.

Unresolved questions needing a design decision:
1. Should the Hunting Spot building scale with the spear research (`hunt_food`), making dailyBuildingEconomy.ts:432 a typo, or is `hunt_yield` an intentional no-op reserved for a different system? This decides whether finding 5 is a bug or just dead code.
2. Is `visitorQuest` meant to be transient? If yes, the simulation should not create it only in the worker (it is currently created by groupEvents in the worker's world and never reaches the main thread) and the UI card should be driven from something that crosses the boundary; if no, it belongs in save + prep + delta.
3. Is the 50-entry `yearlyStats` cap intended to bound lifetime totals? If yes, `totalHumansBorn` needs its own accumulator (finding 6); if the window is only for charts, the lifetime totals should not be derived from it at all.
4. `LeaderPromise`/`WorldState.leaderPromise` is fully unused — confirm it is a removed feature before deleting, since it sits in the save-schema allow-list decision space.
5. Was `EntityCatalog.applyTickDelta` intentionally abandoned in favour of `rebuild`? If so, its doc comment and the delta-sync code should be deleted (it is currently a second, untested path).

Method note: no files were modified; verification was by reading each assigned file in full plus the call sites of every function I could reach a conclusion about (gameTick.ts, saveLoad.ts, viewState.ts, simDelta.ts, simPrep.ts, GameWorkerHost.ts, gameWorker.ts, moonHowler.ts, humanNeeds.ts, dailyBuildingEconomy.ts, grassEcology.ts, worldEvents.ts, entityCounts/entityIndex/entityCatalog callers). No test locks in any of the reported behaviours: grep of C:\Wilderfolk\tests found no coverage of recordYearlyStats/yearlyStats/lifetimeStats totals, entityCounts, playerHuman counts, EntityCatalog, clampToMapBounds, or the research effect targets.
